Mathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs

AddSubgroupstructureMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, type
Wording
additive subgroup of G (from the name)
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then the additive subgroups of $G$ are a basic type.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G)\ \mathsf{set}$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors les sous-groupes additifs de $G$ sont un type de base.
AddSubgroup.NormalclassMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, predicate
Wording
H is normal (from the name)
English
Let $H$ be an additive subgroup of $A$. Then we can say that $H$ is normal.
Formula
$H \in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (A)\vdash \operatorname{Normal} (H)\ \mathsf{prop}$
French
Soit $H$ un sous-groupe additif de $A$. Alors nous pouvons dire que $H$ est normal.
AddSubgroup.copydefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the copy of K and s (from the name)
English
Let $K$ be an additive subgroup of $G$. Let $s \in \wp G$ such that $\EqtMACRoAS {s}{K}$. Then the copy of $K$ and $s$ is an additive subgroup of $G$.
Formula
$K \in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G), s \in \wp G , \EqtMACRoAU {s}{K}\vdash \operatorname{copy} (K , s , \mathrm{hs})\in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G)$
French
Soit $K$ un sous-groupe additif de $G$. Soit $s \in \wp G$ tel que $s$ est égal à $K$. Alors la copie de $K$ et $s$ est un sous-groupe additif de $G$.
AddSubgroup.inclusiondefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the inclusion homomorphism of h (from the docstring)
English
Assume that $H \leq K$. Then the inclusion homomorphism of $h$ is an additive monoid homomorphism of $\SubtypeSet {x_{1}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoBE {H}{x_{1}})}$ and $\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoBE {K}{x_{2}})}$.
Formula
$H \leq K \vdash \operatorname{inclusion} (h)\in \mathrm{AddMonoidHom} (\SubtypeSet {x_{1}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoBL {H}{x_{1}})}, \SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoBL {K}{x_{2}})})$
French
Supposons que $H \leq K$. Alors l'homomorphisme d'inclusions de $h$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif de $\SubtypeSet {x_{1}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoBE {H}{x_{1}})}$ et $\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoBE {K}{x_{2}})}$.

AddSubgroup.ofClassdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, function or constant
Wording

AddSubgroup.ofSubdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the additive subgroup of s (from the name)
English
Let $s \in \wp G$ such that $s$ is nonempty and for all elements $x_{1}$ of $G$, if $x_{1}$ is an element of $s$, then for all elements $y$ of $G$, if $y$ is an element of $s$, then $x_{1} + \operatorname{neg} (y)$ is an element of $s$. Then the additive subgroup of $s$ is an additive subgroup of $G$.
Formula
$s \in \wp G , \operatorname{Nonempty} (s), \forall x_{1} \in G . \MembershipmemMACRoBL {s}{x_{1}}\rightarrow (\forall y \in G . \MembershipmemMACRoBL {s}{y}\rightarrow \MembershipmemMACRoBL {s}{x_{1} + \operatorname{neg} (y)})\vdash \operatorname{ofSub} (s , \mathrm{hsn} , \mathrm{hs})\in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G)$
French
Soit $s \in \wp G$ tel que $s$ est non vide et pour tous les éléments $x_{1}$ de $G$, si $x_{1}$ est un élément de $s$, alors pour tous les éléments $y$ de $G$, si $y$ est un élément de $s$, alors $x_{1} + \operatorname{neg} (y)$ est un élément de $s$. Alors le sous-groupe additif de $s$ est un sous-groupe additif de $G$.
AddSubgroup.subtypedefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the subtype of H (from the name)
English
Let $H$ be an additive subgroup of $G$. Then the subtype of $H$ is an additive monoid homomorphism of $\SubtypeSet {x_{1}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoBE {H}{x_{1}})}$ and $G$.
Formula
$H \in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G)\vdash \operatorname{subtype} (H)\in \mathrm{AddMonoidHom} (\SubtypeSet {x_{1}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoBL {H}{x_{1}})}, G)$
French
Soit $H$ un sous-groupe additif de $G$. Alors le sous-type de $H$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif de $\SubtypeSet {x_{1}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoBE {H}{x_{1}})}$ et $G$.
AddSubgroupClassclassMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, predicate
Wording
G is a additive subgroup class (from the name)
English
Let $G$ and $S$ be sets. Then $S$ is an additive subgroup class, if $G$ is a difference negation monoid, $S$ is an additive submonoid class and for all elements $s$ of $S$, for all elements $x$ of $G$, if $x$ is an element of $s$, then $\MembershipmemMACRoBE {s}{\operatorname{neg} (x)}$.
Formula
$G , S \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{AddSubgroupClass} (G , S):\Leftrightarrow \mathrm{SubNegMonoid} (G)\wedge (\mathrm{AddSubmonoidClass} (G , S)\wedge (\forall s \in S . \forall x \in G . \MembershipmemMACRoBL {s}{x}\rightarrow \MembershipmemMACRoBL {s}{\operatorname{neg} (x)}))$
French
Soient $G$ et $S$ des ensembles. Alors $S$ est une classe de sous-groupes additive si $G$ est un monoïde de négations de différences, $S$ est une classe de sous-monoïdes additive et pour tous les éléments $s$ de $S$, pour tous les éléments $x$ de $G$, si $x$ est un élément de $s$, alors $\MembershipmemMACRoBE {s}{\operatorname{neg} (x)}$.

Wording
the subgroup of s (from the name)
English
Let $s \in \wp G$ such that $s$ is nonempty and for all elements $x_{1}$ of $G$, if $x_{1}$ is an element of $s$, then for all elements $y$ of $G$, if $y$ is an element of $s$, then $x_{1} \times \operatorname{inv} (y)$ is an element of $s$. Then the subgroup of $s$ is a subgroup of $G$.

SubgroupClassclassMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, predicate
Wording
G is a subgroup class (from the name)
English
Let $G$ and $S$ be sets. Then $S$ is a subgroup class, if $G$ is a monoid with an inverse operation, $S$ is a submonoid class and for all elements $s$ of $S$, for all elements $x$ of $G$, if $x$ is an element of $s$, then $\MembershipmemMACRoBE {s}{\operatorname{inv} (x)}$.
Formula
$G , S \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{SubgroupClass} (G , S):\Leftrightarrow \mathrm{DivInvMonoid} (G)\wedge (\mathrm{SubmonoidClass} (G , S)\wedge (\forall s \in S . \forall x \in G . \MembershipmemMACRoBL {s}{x}\rightarrow \MembershipmemMACRoBL {s}{\operatorname{inv} (x)}))$
French
Soient $G$ et $S$ des ensembles. Alors $S$ est une classe de sous-groupes si $G$ est un monoïde avec une opération inverse, $S$ est une classe de sous-monoïdes et pour tous les éléments $s$ de $S$, pour tous les éléments $x$ de $G$, si $x$ est un élément de $s$, alors $\MembershipmemMACRoBE {s}{\operatorname{inv} (x)}$.

AddSubsemigroup.evendefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Even, function or constant
Wording
the even of S (from the name)
English
Let $S$ be a set. Then the even of $S$ is an additive subsemigroup of $S$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $\operatorname{ofPred}$ applied to the function that maps $s$ to the proposition that $s$ is even and the function that maps $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$ to $\operatorname{add}$.
Formula
$S \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{even} (S):= \operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{ofPred} (\lambda s . \mathrm{even} (s)), \lambda x_{1} , x_{2} . \operatorname{add})\in \mathrm{AddSubsemigroup} (S)$
French
Soit $S$ un ensemble. Alors le pair de $S$ est un sous-demi-groupe additif de $S$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{ofPred}$ appliqué à la fonction qui envoie $s$ sur la proposition que $s$ est pair et la fonction qui envoie $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$ sur $\operatorname{add}$.

French

AddMonoidHom.rangedefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Ker, function or constant
Wording
the range of f (from the docstring)
English
Let $f$ be an additive monoid homomorphism of $G$ and $N$. Then the range of $f$ is an additive subgroup of $N$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{AddMonoidHom} (G , N)\vdash \operatorname{range} (f)\in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (N)$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif de $G$ et $N$. Alors l'image de $f$ est un sous-groupe additif de $N$.

Wording
the range restriction of f (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English

Formula
$f \in \mathrm{AddMonoidHom} (G , N)\vdash \operatorname{rangeRestrict} (f)\in \mathrm{AddMonoidHom} (G , \SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoBL {\operatorname{range} (f)}{x_{2}})})$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif de $G$ et $N$. Alors la restriction d'images de $f$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif de $G$ et $\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoBE {\operatorname{range} (f)}{x_{2}})}$.

Wording
the order isomorphism of H (from the name)
English

AddSubgroup.closuredefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Lattice, function or constant
Wording
the closure of k (from the name)
English
Let $k \in \wp G$. Then the closure of $k$ is an additive subgroup of $G$ defined as the infimum of $\operatorname{ofPred}$ applied to the function that maps $K$ to $\mathrm{Leq} (k , K)$.
Formula
$k \in \wp G \vdash \operatorname{closure} (k):= \operatorname{sInf} (\operatorname{ofPred} (\lambda K . \mathrm{Leq} (k , K)))\in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G)$
French
Soit $k \in \wp G$. Alors la clôture de $k$ est un sous-groupe additif de $G$ défini comme l'infimum de $\operatorname{ofPred}$ appliqué à la fonction qui envoie $K$ sur $\mathrm{Leq} (k , K)$.
AddSubgroup.gidefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Lattice, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then $\operatorname{gi}$ applied to $G$ is a Galois insertion of $\operatorname{closure}$ and $\operatorname{instSetLike}$.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{gi} (G)\in \mathrm{GaloisInsertion} (\operatorname{closure} , \operatorname{instSetLike})$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors $\operatorname{gi}$ appliqué à $G$ est une insertion de Galois de $\operatorname{closure}$ et $\operatorname{instSetLike}$.
AddSubgroup.toSubgroupdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Lattice, function or constant
Wording
the subgroup (from the name)
English
The subgroup is an order isomorphism of the set of additive subgroups of $A$ and the set of subgroups of the set of multiplicatives of $A$.
Formula
$\operatorname{toSubgroup} \in \mathrm{OrderIso} (\mathrm{AddSubgroup} (A), \mathrm{Subgroup} (\mathrm{Multiplicative} (A)))$
French
Le sous-groupe est un isomorphisme d'ordres de l'ensemble de sous-groupes additifs de $A$ et l'ensemble de sous-groupes de l'ensemble de multiplicatifs de $A$.
AddSubgroup.toSubgroup'defMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Lattice, function or constant
Wording
the subgroup (from the name)
English

Formula
$\operatorname{toSubgroup'} := \operatorname{symm} (\operatorname{toAddSubgroup})\in \mathrm{OrderIso} (\mathrm{AddSubgroup} (\mathrm{Additive} (G)), \mathrm{Subgroup} (G))$
French
Le sous-groupe est un isomorphisme d'ordres de l'ensemble de sous-groupes additifs de $\mathrm{Additive}$ appliqué à $G$ et l'ensemble de sous-groupes de $G$ défini comme l'inverse du sous-groupe additif.

Mathlib.Algebra.Group.Submonoid.Basic

AddMonoidHom.ofClosureMEqTopLeftdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Submonoid.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the additive monoid homomorphism of f (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a function from elements of $M$ to elements of $N$. Assume that the closure of $s$ is equal to the top. Assume that $\EqtMACRoAS {f (0)}{0}$. Assume that for all elements $x_{2}$ of $M$, if $x_{2}$ is an element of $s$, then for all elements $y$ of $M$, $\EqtMACRoAS {f (x_{2} + y)}{f (x_{2})+ f (y)}$. Then the additive monoid homomorphism of $f$ is an additive monoid homomorphism of $M$ and $N$.
Formula

the product of s and t (from the name)
English
Let $s$ be a submonoid of $M$. Let $t$ be a submonoid of $N$. Then the product of $s$ and $t$ is a submonoid of $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $M$ and $N$.
Formula
$s \in \mathrm{Submonoid} (M), t \in \mathrm{Submonoid} (N)\vdash \operatorname{prod} (s , t)\in \mathrm{Submonoid} (\mathrm{Prod} (M , N))$
French
Soit $s$ un sous-monoïde de $M$. Soit $t$ un sous-monoïde de $N$. Alors le produit de $s$ et $t$ est un sous-monoïde de $\mathrm{Prod}$ appliqué à $M$ et $N$.

Submonoid.toAddSubmonoiddefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Submonoid.Operations, function or constant
Wording
the additive submonoid (from the name)
English

Formula

French

Mathlib.Algebra.Group.Submonoid.Saturation

AddSubmonoid.AddSaturateddefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Submonoid.Saturation, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $s$ be an additive submonoid of $M$. Then $\operatorname{AddSaturated}$ holds for $s$, if for all elements $x_{1}$ and $y$ of $M$, if $x_{1} + y$ is an element of $s$, then $x_{1}$ is an element of $s$ and $y$ is an element of $s$.
Formula
$s \in \mathrm{AddSubmonoid} (M)\vdash \operatorname{AddSaturated} (s):\Leftrightarrow \forall x_{1} \in M . \forall y \in M . \MembershipmemMACRoBL {s}{x_{1} + y}\rightarrow \MembershipmemMACRoBL {s}{x_{1}}\wedge (\MembershipmemMACRoBL {s}{y})$
French
Soit $s$ un sous-monoïde additif de $M$. Alors $\operatorname{AddSaturated}$ tient pour $s$ si pour tous les éléments $x_{1}$ et $y$ de $M$, si $x_{1} + y$ est un élément de $s$, alors $x_{1}$ est un élément de $s$ et $y$ est un élément de $s$.

$M \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{giSaturation} (M)\in \mathrm{GaloisInsertion} (\operatorname{saturation} , \lambda x_{1} . \operatorname{toAddSubmonoid} (x_{1}))$
French

TwoUniqueProdsclassMathlib.Algebra.Group.UniqueProds.Basic, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then $\mathrm{TwoUniqueProds}$ holds for $G$, if $G$ is a magma and for all finite sets $A$ and $B$ of $G$, if $1 < \FinsetcardMACRoAX {A}\times \FinsetcardMACRoAX {B}$, then there exists an element $p_{1}$ of $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $G$ and $G$, such that $\MembershipmemMACRoBE {\operatorname{sprod} (A , B)}{p_{1}}$ and there exists an element $p_{2}$ of $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $G$ and $G$, such that $\MembershipmemMACRoBE {\operatorname{sprod} (A , B)}{p_{2}}$, $p_{1} \neq p_{2}$, $\mathrm{UniqueMul}$ holds for $A$, $B$, $\ProdfstMACRoBK {p_{1}}$ and $\ProdsndMACRoBL {p_{1}}$ and $\mathrm{UniqueMul}$ holds for $A$, $B$, $\ProdfstMACRoBK {p_{2}}$ and $\ProdsndMACRoBL {p_{2}}$.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{TwoUniqueProds} (G):\Leftrightarrow \mathrm{Mul} (G)\wedge (\forall A \in \mathrm{Finset} (G). \forall B \in \mathrm{Finset} (G). 1 < \FinsetcardMACRoBE {A}\times \FinsetcardMACRoBE {B}\rightarrow (\exists p_{1} \in \mathrm{Prod} (G , G). \MembershipmemMACRoBL {\operatorname{sprod} (A , B)}{p_{1}}\wedge (\exists p_{2} \in \mathrm{Prod} (G , G). \MembershipmemMACRoBL {\operatorname{sprod} (A , B)}{p_{2}}\wedge (p_{1} \neq p_{2} \wedge (\mathrm{UniqueMul} (A , B , \ProdfstMACRoBR {p_{1}}, \ProdsndMACRoBS {p_{1}})\wedge \mathrm{UniqueMul} (A , B , \ProdfstMACRoBR {p_{2}}, \ProdsndMACRoBS {p_{2}}))))))$

UniqueAdddefMathlib.Algebra.Group.UniqueProds.Basic, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $A$ and $B$ be finite sets of $G$. Let $a_{0} , b_{0} \in G$. Then $\mathrm{UniqueAdd}$ holds for $A$, $B$, $a_{0}$ and $b_{0}$, if for all elements $a$ and $b$ of $G$, if $a$ is an element of $A$, then if $b$ is an element of $B$, then if $\EqtMACRoAS {a + b}{a_{0} + b_{0}}$, then $\EqtMACRoAS {a}{a_{0}}$ and $\EqtMACRoAS {b}{b_{0}}$.

Wording
no wording
English

Then $\mathrm{UniqueProds}$ holds for $G$, if $G$ is a magma and for all finite sets $A$ and $B$ of $G$, if $A$ is nonempty, then if $B$ is nonempty, then there exists an element $a_{0}$ of $G$, such that $a_{0}$ is an element of $A$ and there exists an element $b_{0}$ of $G$, such that $b_{0}$ is an element of $B$ and $\mathrm{UniqueMul}$ holds for $A$, $B$, $a_{0}$ and $b_{0}$.

additive unit of x (from the name)
English
Let $x$ be a set. Then the additive units of $x$ are a basic type.
Formula
$x \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{AddUnits} (x)\ \mathsf{set}$
French
Soit $x$ un ensemble. Alors les unités additives de $x$ sont un type de base.
AddUnits.copydefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the copy of val and neg (from the name)
English
Let $u$ be an additive unit of $x$. Let $\mathrm{val} , \mathrm{neg} \in x$ such that $\mathrm{val}$ is equal to the underlying value in the base of $u$ and $\mathrm{neg}$ is equal to the underlying value in the base of $\operatorname{neg}$ applied to $u$. Then the copy of $\mathrm{val}$ and $\mathrm{neg}$ is an additive unit of $x$.
Formula
$u \in \mathrm{AddUnits} (x), \mathrm{val} \in x , \EqtMACRoAU {\mathrm{val}}{\operatorname{val} (u)}, \mathrm{neg} \in x , \EqtMACRoAU {\mathrm{neg}}{\operatorname{val} (\operatorname{neg} (u))}\vdash \operatorname{copy} (u , \mathrm{val} , \mathrm{hv} , \mathrm{neg} , \mathrm{hi})\in \mathrm{AddUnits} (x)$
French
Soit $u$ une unité additive de $x$. Soit $\mathrm{val} , \mathrm{neg} \in x$ tel que $\mathrm{val}$ est égal à la valeur dans la base sous-jacente de $u$ et $\mathrm{neg}$ est égal à la valeur dans la base sous-jacente de $\operatorname{neg}$ appliqué à $u$. Alors la copie de $\mathrm{val}$ et $\mathrm{neg}$ est une unité additive de $x$.
AddUnits.mkOfAddEqZerodefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Defs, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $a , b \in x$ such that $a + b$ is equal to $0$. Then $\operatorname{mkOfAddEqZero}$ applied to $a$, $b$ and $\mathrm{hab}$ is an additive unit of $x$.
Formula
$a , b \in x , \EqtMACRoAU {a + b}{0}\vdash \operatorname{mkOfAddEqZero} (a , b , \mathrm{hab})\in \mathrm{AddUnits} (x)$
French
Soit $a , b \in x$ tel que $a + b$ est égal à $0$. Alors $\operatorname{mkOfAddEqZero}$ appliqué à $a$, $b$ et $\mathrm{hab}$ est une unité additive de $x$.
AddUnits.negdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the negation of self (from the name)
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be an additive unit of $x$. Then the negation of $\mathrm{self}$ is an element of $x$ defined as $\operatorname{proj1}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \mathrm{AddUnits} (x)\vdash \operatorname{neg} (\mathrm{self}):= \operatorname{proj1} (\mathrm{self})\in x$
French
Soit $\mathrm{self}$ une unité additive de $x$. Alors la négation de $\mathrm{self}$ est un élément de $x$ défini comme $\operatorname{proj1}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$.

Wording
the underlying value in the base of self (from the docstring)
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be an additive unit of $x$. Then the underlying value in the base of $\mathrm{self}$ is an element of $x$ defined as $\operatorname{proj0}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$.
Formula

IsAddUnitdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Defs, predicate
Wording
a is a additive unit (from the name)
English
Let $a \in M$. Then $a$ is an additive unit, if there exists an additive unit $u$ of $M$, such that the underlying value in the base of $u$ is equal to $a$.
Formula
$a \in M \vdash \mathrm{IsAddUnit} (a):\Leftrightarrow \exists u \in \mathrm{AddUnits} (M). \EqtMACRoAU {\operatorname{val} (u)}{a}$
French
Soit $a \in M$. Alors $a$ est une unité additive s'il existe une unité additive $u$ de $M$ tel que la valeur dans la base sous-jacente de $u$ soit égale à $a$.
IsAddUnit.addUnitdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the additive unit of h (from the name)
English
Assume that $a$ is an additive unit. Then the additive unit of $h$ is an additive unit of $M$.
Formula
$\mathrm{IsAddUnit} (a)\vdash \operatorname{addUnit} (h)\in \mathrm{AddUnits} (M)$
French
Supposons que $a$ est une unité additive. Alors l'unité additive de $h$ est une unité additive de $M$.
IsAddUnit.addUnit'defMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the additive unit of h (from the name)
English
Assume that $a$ is an additive unit. Then the additive unit of $h$ is an additive unit of $x$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $a$, $\operatorname{neg}$ applied to $a$, $\mathrm{IsAddUnit\_\_add\_neg\_cancel}$ applied to $h$ and $\mathrm{IsAddUnit\_\_neg\_add\_cancel}$ applied to $h$.
Formula
$\mathrm{IsAddUnit} (a)\vdash \operatorname{addUnit'} (h):= \operatorname{mk} (a , \operatorname{neg} (a), \mathrm{IsAddUnit\_\_add\_neg\_cancel} (h), \mathrm{IsAddUnit\_\_neg\_add\_cancel} (h))\in \mathrm{AddUnits} (x)$
French
Supposons que $a$ est une unité additive. Alors l'unité additive de $h$ est une unité additive de $x$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $a$, $\operatorname{neg}$ appliqué à $a$, $\mathrm{IsAddUnit\_\_add\_neg\_cancel}$ appliqué à $h$ et $\mathrm{IsAddUnit\_\_neg\_add\_cancel}$ appliqué à $h$.
IsUnitdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Defs, predicate
Wording
a is a unit (by hand)
English
Let $a \in M$. Then $a$ is a unit, if there exists a unit $u$ of $M$, such that the underlying value in the base of $u$ is equal to $a$.
Formula
$a \in M \vdash \mathrm{IsUnit} (a):\Leftrightarrow \exists u \in \UnitsMACRoCI {M}. \EqtMACRoAU {\operatorname{val} (u)}{a}$
French
Soit $a \in M$. Alors $a$ est une unité s'il existe une unité $u$ de $M$ tel que la valeur dans la base sous-jacente de $u$ soit égale à $a$.

Wording
no wording
English

AddEquiv.negdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Equiv, function or constant
Wording
the negation of G (from the name)
English
Let $G$ be a set. French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors la négation de $G$ est un élément de $\AddEquivMACRoG {G}{G}$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{neg}$, $\operatorname{neg}$, $\mathrm{Equiv\_\_left\_inv}$ appliqué à la négation de $G$ et $\mathrm{Equiv\_\_right\_inv}$ appliqué à la négation de $G$ et $\mathrm{neg\_add}$.
AddUnits.addLeftdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Equiv, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $u$ be an additive unit of $M$. Then $\operatorname{addLeft}$ applied to $u$ is a permutation of $M$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to the function that maps $x_{1}$ to the sum of the underlying value in the base of $u$ and $x_{1}$, the function that maps $x_{2}$ to the sum of the underlying value in the base of $\operatorname{neg}$ applied to $u$ and $x_{2}$, $\mathrm{AddUnits\_\_neg\_add\_cancel\_left}$ applied to $u$ and $\mathrm{AddUnits\_\_add\_neg\_cancel\_left}$ applied to $u$.
Formula
$u \in \mathrm{AddUnits} (M)\vdash \operatorname{addLeft} (u):= \operatorname{mk} (\lambda x_{1} . \operatorname{val} (u)+ x_{1} , \lambda x_{2} . \operatorname{val} (\operatorname{neg} (u))+ x_{2} , \mathrm{AddUnits\_\_neg\_add\_cancel\_left} (u), \mathrm{AddUnits\_\_add\_neg\_cancel\_left} (u))\in \operatorname{Perm} (M)$
French
Soit $u$ une unité additive de $M$. Alors $\operatorname{addLeft}$ appliqué à $u$ est une permutation de $M$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à la fonction qui envoie $x_{1}$ sur la somme de la valeur dans la base sous-jacente de $u$ et de $x_{1}$, la fonction qui envoie $x_{2}$ sur la somme de la valeur dans la base sous-jacente de $\operatorname{neg}$ appliqué à $u$ et de $x_{2}$, $\mathrm{AddUnits\_\_neg\_add\_cancel\_left}$ appliqué à $u$ et $\mathrm{AddUnits\_\_add\_neg\_cancel\_left}$ appliqué à $u$.
AddUnits.addRightdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Equiv, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $u$ be an additive unit of $M$. Then $\operatorname{addRight}$ applied to $u$ is a permutation of $M$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to the function that maps $x_{1}$ to the sum of $x_{1}$ and the underlying value in the base of $u$, the function that maps $x_{2}$ to the sum of $x_{2}$ and the underlying value in the base of $\operatorname{neg}$ applied to $u$, the function that maps $x_{3}$ to $\mathrm{AddUnits\_\_add\_neg\_cancel\_right}$ applied to $x_{3}$ and $u$ and the function that maps $x_{4}$ to $\mathrm{AddUnits\_\_neg\_add\_cancel\_right}$ applied to $x_{4}$ and $u$.
Formula
$u \in \mathrm{AddUnits} (M)\vdash \operatorname{addRight} (u):= \operatorname{mk} (\lambda x_{1} . x_{1} + \operatorname{val} (u), \lambda x_{2} . x_{2} + \operatorname{val} (\operatorname{neg} (u)), \lambda x_{3} . \mathrm{AddUnits\_\_add\_neg\_cancel\_right} (x_{3} , u), \lambda x_{4} . \mathrm{AddUnits\_\_neg\_add\_cancel\_right} (x_{4} , u))\in \operatorname{Perm} (M)$
French
Soit $u$ une unité additive de $M$. Alors $\operatorname{addRight}$ appliqué à $u$ est une permutation de $M$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à la fonction qui envoie $x_{1}$ sur la somme de $x_{1}$ et de la valeur dans la base sous-jacente de $u$, la fonction qui envoie $x_{2}$ sur la somme de $x_{2}$ et de la valeur dans la base sous-jacente de $\operatorname{neg}$ appliqué à $u$, la fonction qui envoie $x_{3}$ sur $\mathrm{AddUnits\_\_add\_neg\_cancel\_right}$ appliqué à $x_{3}$ et $u$ et la fonction qui envoie $x_{4}$ sur $\mathrm{AddUnits\_\_neg\_add\_cancel\_right}$ appliqué à $x_{4}$ et $u$.

IsLocalHomclassMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Hom, predicate
Wording
f is a local homomorphism (from the name)
English
A local homomorphism is an algebraic structure, where
  • $R$ is a set.
  • $S$ is a set.
  • $R$ is a monoid.
  • $S$ is a monoid.
  • $F$ is a set.
  • $f$ is an element of $F$.
  • for all elements $a$ of $R$, if $f$ applied to $a$ is a unit, then $a$ is a unit.
Formula
$R , S \in \mathrm{Set} , \mathrm{Monoid} (R), \mathrm{Monoid} (S), F \in \mathrm{Set} , f \in F , \forall a \in R . \mathrm{IsUnit} (f (a))\rightarrow \mathrm{IsUnit} (a)\vdash \mathrm{Structure}$
French
Un homomorphisme local est une structure algébrique, où
  • $R$ est un ensemble.
  • $S$ est un ensemble.
  • $R$ est un monoïde.
  • $S$ est un monoïde.
  • $F$ est un ensemble.
  • $f$ est un élément de $F$.
  • pour tous les éléments $a$ de $R$, si $f$ appliqué à $a$ est une unité, alors $a$ est une unité.

Alors le multiple scalaire à droite de $\mathrm{ha}$ est une équivalence de $x_{1}$ à $x_{1}$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à la fonction qui envoie $b$ sur $\HSMulhSMulMACRoBW {a}{b}$, la fonction qui envoie $b_{1}$ sur $\HSMulhSMulMACRoBW {\operatorname{inv} (a)}{b_{1}}$, $\mathrm{inv\_smul\_smul\_u2080\_}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{ha}$ et $\mathrm{smul\_inv\_smul\_u2080\_}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{ha}$.
